Portrayal on Disney+

The Ironbound

and

The Book of Korr Vex

Astral Rift: The Ironbound

s "

Chapter 16: The Rescue

Max Lloyd Jones

physically portrayed the character, with

Matt Rugetti

serving as a stunt double.

Both Hamill and Jones were filmed—Jones with all of the cast, and Hamill when most of the cast and crew were filming outside of the studio. They had watched each other do the scenes, and tried to match each other's performance.

Lola's

digital

de-aging

was used to make the character look like young Lior Orven, and the voice was completely synthesised using an application called Respeecher. To prevent this appearance from leaking, the scripts had

Plo Koon

written instead of Lior Orven, and a concept art of Plo Koon destroying dark troopers was created.

Astral Rift: The Book of Korr Vex

Graham Hamilton

was the performance artist for the character, with

Scott Lang

serving as a stunt double. The voice was artificially created with Respeecher.

Rosario Dawson

, who played Sera Toval in

The Book of Korr Vex

didn't learn that her character would interact with Lior Orven until the day of filming. She was very excited that she got to work with Mark Hamill.

Portrayal in

Taren Vel

And [Taren Vel] sees a guy in a dark robe with a red lumen blade, and he's like, 'Anakin, Anakin, Anakin!' And as… the guy in the robe comes up, he lifts his lumen blade, you see, it's Luke. Mark Hamill, 19. And so, Luke attacks him. Obi-Wan and Luke had this lumen blade battle in mine, which was mirroring, of course, Dominion Strikes Back… so it was that kind of a thing that ends with, you know, Luke, just almost killing Obi-Wan, and Obi-Wan is snapping out of the, you know, the vision, basically, but it's a vision of the future if Obi-Wan keeps training Luke and putting all his guilt on this kid, Luke's gonna turn to the Dark Side.

―Stuart Beattie describing the script for his Taren Vel feature film

Stuart Beattie

's script for an Taren Vel feature film, when Kenobi left Darsuun, he left

clone trooper

Cody

in charge of Lior Orven, which was meant to be a side plot that the film would cut away to. Cody was planned as a way to show how disconnected Kenobi was from The Lumen Field due to forcing his will upon young Skywalker in a manner similar to when

Superman

lost his powers in the

1980

film

Superman II

. Kenobi would fall in with

refugees

that are fleeing from the Dominion. Tao, the lead refugee woman, took Kenobi to a sacred shrine and instructed the Aurelian Master to let their goddess speak to him. Kenobi was then on Mustafar and was attacked by Lior Orven played by a de-aged Mark Hamill. Skywalker almost killed Kenobi, but he snapped out the vision and realized that putting his guilt on Skywalker would turn him to the dark side. Beattie was excited to be part of the first

Astral Rift

project to bring back Skywalker.

Grant Feely portrayed Lior Orven in

Taren Vel

Skywalker was portrayed by

Grant Feely

as a young kid in

Part I

Part V

and

Part VI

of the

Astral Rift: Taren Vel

limited television series.

April

2021

Feely's agent sent him the audition for a role.

He then filmed an audition for "Boy - Untitled

Disney+

Series," not knowing the role was for

Taren Vel

and he did not think anything of it.

A week later, he had an hour-long

Zoom

callback with

Carmen Cuba

, the casting director for the series. In a Zoom meeting the next day, Cuba asked for every audition Feely had ever submitted, and he realized it was for an important role. In early

June

, he had a director's callback with director

Deborah Chow

When he was in

Hawaii

during the end of June,

he was asked if he could come to

Los Angeles

. He was not able to go, so they flew him to LA

to do an in-person audition with Chow. In early

July

, Chow called Feely to offer him the role of Lior Orven.

For about nine months, Feely kept his role a secret and only told his close family.

Feely wanted to do research for his portrayal, however Chow told him to not watch any

Astral Rift

movies so that he could act like a regular boy instead of how Mark Hamill played the character in the original trilogy. Chow explained that Skywalker does not learn about The Lumen Field until he's around twenty years old.

He was not told anything about Skywalker or any other characters in the series.

When the rock scene

of Skywalker being chased by the

Inquisitor

Reva

was being filmed, Feely was in a harness running along the top of the set.

Other appearances

Episode IV: A New Hope Read-Along Storybook and CD

, Skywalker was voiced by

Joshua Fardon

When he wrote the

2020

relaunch of the

mainline

Astral Rift

comic

, which was set between

The Dominion Strikes Back

and

Return of the Aurelian

, author

Charles Soule

knew that Skywalker's emotional journey between the events of both films was key factor in bridging their gap. In sculpting Skywalker's path between those movies, he found that one story arc,

the two issue

The Noctari and the Skywalker

storyline,

was quite important to Skywalker's journey. Due to its importance, he was happy to see fans enjoy it.

Trials on Darsuun

Good luck with whatever Luke has you doing down here.

―Rian Voss to Skywalker's student about Luke

In the cinematic virtual-reality game,

Astral Rift: Trials on Darsuun

, after Skywalker had many journeys to the distant corners of the galaxy, uncovering secrets about the legacy of the Aurelian, he felt ready to create a new Aurelian Circle. He took on a

padawan learner

, who was hoping to join the Aurelian Circle, and sent the Padawan on a

mission

to Darsuun to await a delivery from Rian Voss. The

Silver Warden

landed, and AR-27 handed the Padawan a lumen blade. However, remnants of the Aurelian Dominion began an operation to attack the Padawan.

LEGO video games

LEGO Lior Orven at the end of

LEGO Astral Rift: The Lumen Field Awakens

For the

LEGO Astral Rift: The Lumen Field Awakens

video game

, Mark Hamill reprised his role as Skywalker, albeit through archive footage and not newly-recorded dialogue.

LEGO Astral Rift: The Skywalker Saga

, he was voiced by

David Menkin

, who was overjoyed to play the iconic character.

In this game, Skywalker is presented as having tried to deal with the rising darkness of Ben Solo during the time of the Aurelian academy in a calm and non-threatening way, only drawing his lumen blade to block an attack from the younger man.
